One convention to note: `&` pitches the heading away from the up vector (down, as named), the opposite of the literal R_L(delta) matrix in the book, whose naming is itself inverted; the doc comment states the behaviour. Second pass: (1) trees kernel: the published rule regrows the same child every round while the points that pulled it stay nearer the parent (each less than D/2 along the growth direction), so the default crown of seed 1 had 315 of its 682 nodes as exact copies of a sibling (trees-kernel/src/lib.rs growth loop); (2) tree-mesh: the base cap of each bark tube stored tangent w = +1 for a mirrored UV disc (36 of 36 base-cap vertices wrong on a 12-segment tube); (3) plants: trunk() put its first radius at the root, and every caller passed the tip first, so palm, birch, acacia and snag trunks and limbs widened toward the top (palm 0.13 m at the root, 0.22 m under the crown); (4) cactus ribs were computed about the world Y axis after placement, which sheared the arms (arm radius 0.166 m where 0.148 is the most a 6 percent rib allows). Third pass: (5) building shells had T junctions at every window, floor line, base and roof edge (default building: 428 of 1,272 directed edges unmatched; pinholes when rasterised); (6) SDF dual quads always split on one diagonal, folding triangles inward at CSG seams (87 in 300 random two-sphere unions). Fourth pass: (7) marchingCubes made one vertex per grid edge, so a surface through a grid point put a vertex on it per edge (sphere radius 0.5 at resolution 13: 252 of 888 triangles of zero area), and oriented faces from a triangle corner that could be that point; (8) the tree stand-in's canopy wound clockwise from outside and its trunk was two one-sided quads, so a single-sided far forest drew each crown from inside and no trunk from half the directions, and an inherited key ('constructor') passed its kind check; (9) the SDF grid point open from the third pass: a pole rounding to -1e-16 counted as inside. Third pass: (5) every face cut at the same columns and rows, caps and roofs fanned over them (test/buildings.test.js "the shell is closed"); (6) the split keeps the diagonal whose triangles both face along the quad's edge (test/sdf.test.js "no folded triangles"; 8 left at sharp seams, docs/limits.md). (2) XML: `&#0;`, surrogates and U+FFFE were accepted as character references (2.2 forbids them). (3) XML: attribute values kept literal tabs and line ends (3.3.3 makes them spaces) and CR LF reached text as two characters (2.11). (4) XML: `encoding="ISO-8859-1"` bytes were decoded as UTF-8, turning every accented letter into U+FFFD (4.3.3). (5) ZIP: names without bit 11 were decoded as UTF-8; APPNOTE appendix D says IBM Code Page 437, so a `café` folder from an old zip tool read as U+FFFD. (6) Shapefile MultiPatch: vertical walls (zero x/y area) triangulated to nothing, and rings were grouped by x/y containment instead of the Outer/Inner and First/Ring sequences the description defines. (7) Docs: parseLAS said LAS 1.0 to 1.4 (1.5 has read since the points audit); createPBRMeshes said parts split at 1,000,000 vertices (262,144). (8) AMF: a volume naming a material took the material colour over its object colour (52915 ranks material lowest), and triangle colours were not read at all. (9) VTK: higher-order cell types 26 to 37 and the Lagrange and Bezier cells 68 to 81 were dropped as not drawn, though their corners come first and the linear faces apply (types 21 to 25 already drew). (10) G-code: the filament doc said the sum of positive E moves; the code sums every move, the net, which is right. (11) 3DM: unit numbers 6, 7, 12, 13 and 19 to 25 mapped to the wrong scale (6 read as angstroms). (12) DXF: $INSUNITS 21 to 24 (US survey foot, inch, yard, mile) read as unitless. (14) MaterialX: a nested fileprefix was appended to the outer one; the specification says the inner one replaces it. (15) SVG: a nested svg or a symbol without width and height kept its viewBox size instead of 100% of the viewport (SVG 2 5.6, 8.2). (21) USD: a material:binding on an ancestor prim did not reach the meshes under it. (22) USD: prims with purpose guide were drawn. (27) VTK legacy (parseVTK, parseVTKLegacy): TEXTURE_COORDINATES were not turned to v = 1 - t, so textures drew upside down against parseVTKXML. Second pass: expandLine (geometry.ts) against the vertex shader corner by corner: body corners, the two gap triangles of a join block (inner, centre, outer with d along nrm then n1), bevel (centre, outer0, outer1 at d = hwG), round fans of FAN = 10 triangles from outer0 by the signed angle, cap fans from nrm by -pi at the end and +pi at the start, cap extension (square hw + aa, butt aa) and the zero-area filter: the mirror matches; the miter test L = 1 / cos(theta / 2) is SVG's miter ratio 1 / sin(phi / 2); computeLineDistances (segments, strips, a loop's closing length); Lines: uniform change detection, depth compares under reversed Z (behind = less, depth-tested = greater-equal), the two draw steps of a depth-writing line (cover >= 0.5 writes depth, the ramp blends), 6 vertices for joinless non-round segments; LineBatch packing (per point or per segment colours, sRGB bytes decoded in the shader, dirty ranges split at maxBufferSize); helper bases (u = r x d, v = d x u right-handed, the +Z switch past |y| 0.999), BoxHelper's eight transformed corners, VertexNormalsHelper's inverse transpose as (a1 x a2, a2 x a0, a0 x a1) times sign(det), CameraHelper's reversed-Z unprojection (near z 1, far z 0, finite far enforced by core); light helpers (spot inner angle angle (1 - penumbra), 85 degree clamp); Trail's ring buffer, age and length cuts interpolated inside the last kept step and the samples past an age cut dropped; arcs (parabolic rise 4 h t (1 - t), great circle with linear radius); DebugLines queue per width and depth, flushed once per rendered frame | Second pass: a repeated point in a strip (or two points on one pixel) drops the join there: with butt caps the miter, bevel or round corner is left uncovered, with round caps a miter or bevel join draws round (expandLine and the shader alike; measured on an up-then-left corner). it fails all 13 without the destroy. meshopt.ts and the meshopt-simd crate: the version 1 attribute stream, channel modes, filters (octahedral, quaternion, exponential, colour, equal within the specification's 1 ULP); lossless files read exactly as their sources (strips, tiles, Predictor 2 at 8 and 16 bits, Predictor 3 on floats), lossy WebP within 1 of `tiff2rgba`; openTIFF pages read CCITT, JPEG and FillOrder 2. vp8.ts read line by line against RFC 6386 (Bankoski, Koleszar, Quillio, Salonen, Wilkins and Xu, 2011, https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc6386, fetched and read in sections 7 to 15 and 19): the frame header order, segment and loop filter deltas, token partitions, the coefficient token tree, the extra-bit categories and their probabilities, the band and context rules (Y2 neighbours taken from the last macroblock with a Y2 block), the 10 subblock and 4 macroblock predictors with the 127 and 129 frame edges and the above-right rule, the simple, subblock and macroblock filters, the interior, edge and high-variance limits and the filter order, no defect; 28 cwebp 1.6.0 files (quality 0 to 100, methods 0 to 6, 1 to 4 segments, sharpness 0 to 7, filter strength 0 to 100, simple and normal filters, two source images) decode to dwebp's Y, U and V planes byte for byte, two of them kept as fixtures (lossy-sharpness-1, lossy-sharpness-7). cwebp exposes no token partition count, so files with 2, 4 or 8 partitions were not run. The meshopt index codecs (meshoptDecodeIndexBuffer, meshoptDecodeIndexSequence and the crate's decode_triangles and decode_sequence) read line by line against KHR_meshopt_compression Appendix A modes 1 and 2 and EXT_meshopt_compression (https://github.com/KhronosGroup/glTF/blob/main/extensions/2.0/Vendor/EXT_meshopt_compression/README.md, fetched and read 2026-11-16): the code table, FIFO order, `next` reset, zigzag deltas and both baselines match; header version 0 (an older encoder's form, outside both specifications) stays accepted. ktx2-layers.ts read line by line: every VkFormat number against the Vulkan enumeration (9 to 184, the ASTC block order included), the RGB to RGBA expansion and level sizes; BC1 RGB (131, 132) reported alpha, fixed below. ktx2-gpu.ts read line by line (band split, row alignment, copy extents, image order of BasisLZ slices, sRGB from the DFD transfer byte); its texels equal the Wasm transcoder's on 11 real files on WebGPU (tests/decoders-browser.js). The layer count was never checked against the device, fixed below. texture.ts and ktx2-stream.ts read for the same limits: `createKTX2Texture` checked no `maxTextureDimension2D`, and the streamer neither that nor `maxTextureArrayLayers`, fixed below. ktx2-gpu-shaders.ts read line by line against *UASTC LDR 4x4 Texture Specification* (Binomial, public domain, https://github.com/BinomialLLC/basis_universal/wiki/UASTC-LDR-4x4-Texture-Specification, fetched and read 2026-11-16, spec text only): the 128-entry mode table, every mode's field offsets, weight bits, subsets, components and planes, the endpoint ranges and their trit and quint packing (the shortest code for a short last group), the 60 partition patterns and anchors (compared by script, each anchor the first texel of its subset), the void-extent layout, LLLA for the LA modes, CCS 3 for mode 17, anchors in both planes, interpolation with the sRGB flag off as the reference encoder sets it; the endpoint unquantisation and weight tables against the ASTC section of the Khronos Data Format Specification. A PAT value past its mode's table was decoded from the neighbouring table, fixed below. ktx2-hdr.ts, ktx2-hdr6.ts and ktx2-hdr6-codec.ts read line by line: the half-float table (IEEE 754 binary16), the cube face split, VK_FORMAT_ASTC_6x6_SFLOAT_BLOCK (1000066004), the DFD colour model (162 ASTC), transfer (linear) and block dimensions (5, 5) at their KTX 2.0 offsets, the ASTC level size against its declared length, and the 6x6 intermediate seek table (12 bytes an image, level-major) against *UASTC HDR 6x6 Support Notes* (Binomial, https://github.com/BinomialLLC/basis_universal/wiki/UASTC-HDR-6x6-Support-Notes, fetched 2026-11-16); the intermediate format's own description is not published (the notes say its header IDs are still being settled with Khronos), so the clean-room crate stays verified by its differential test against the independent transcoder, no defect. jxl-file.ts, jxl.ts, jxl-render.ts and jxl.worker.ts read line by line. ISO/IEC 18181-1 and 18181-2 are sold, not public: the 2019 committee draft (arXiv 1908.03565) and the 2021 FDIS preview (iTeh sample) were fetched and read in the parts they hold: least-significant-bit-first order (FDIS 6.3), U32 fields and SizeHeader with its aspect ratios (CD A.4.2.2, unchanged); the CD's ImageMetadata, ExtraChannelInfo and ColourEncoding layouts predate the final text, so those fields rest on the existing differential against `jxlinfo` over nine conformance files (tests/jxl.test.js). The container signature was checked against the IANA image/jxl registration (2024, magic numbers): the reader checked the box size and type but not its content 0D 0A 87 0A, fixed below. The jxlp ordering rules of 18181-2 are not public and not checked. lzfse.ts: Apple publishes no LZFSE specification; the decoder is built from Metz's Keramics format descriptions, Hron's thesis and Duda's ANS paper (docs/clean-room.md) and verified by exact output on seven fixtures compressed by Apple's reference codec (the prebuilt lzfse 0.4.2.post1 Python wheel, THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md: raw, LZVN, v1, v2, reused tables, several blocks, entropy-rich), truncation at every byte and the mutation fuzz above. | KTX `COMPRESSED_RGB_S3TC_DXT1` (0x83f0) and its sRGB form (0x8c4c) reported `hasAlpha: true`; those formats have no alpha, and WebGPU's bc1-rgba-unorm samples a three-colour block's black texel with alpha 0. The meshopt version 1 decoders (TypeScript and the Wasm SIMD crate) checked only `(mode & 3) === 3`: KHR_meshopt_compression makes a low nibble of 3 to 15 invalid, and modes 0 and 1 with a set high nibble too, so a mode byte 0x04 decoded as byte deltas, 0x05 as 2-byte deltas and 0x10 was accepted, and a stream with no elements never checked its modes at all. KTX2 Zstandard levels were never compared with their declared uncompressedByteLength (the zlib path was), so a short level reached the transcoder short. readKTX2Index bounded levelCount by max(width, height), refusing valid 3D files whose depth is the largest axis. openTIFF read subsampled YCbCr pages outside JPEG as if their Section 21 data units were interleaved pixels. geotiff.js lists LERC, which has no public bitstream specification: refused, docs/known-bugs.md. | `hasAlpha` is false for the two RGB DXT1 numbers, with a regression test over every compressed format and a README line. Both meshopt decoders refuse every invalid channel mode, with or without elements (tests/meshopt.test.js, tests/meshopt-simd.test.js); a Zstandard KTX2 level of the wrong length fails as damaged, and levelCount is bounded by the largest of width, height and depth (tests/decoders.test.js). Each test fails on the code before its fix. Subsampled YCbCr raw pages are refused outside JPEG (tests/tiff-libtiff.test.js, fails on the old source); LZMA, Zstandard and WebP TIFFs are read by both readers through decoders `loadTIFFCodecs` (`/tiff-codecs`) loads on demand, so the `/tiff` entry and the FBX worker do not carry them. Both meshopt index decoders refuse a FIFO slot read before it was written, a codeaux table with a 0xf nibble or non-zero last two bytes, and a varint past 5 bytes, where they decoded such streams before (tests/meshopt.test.js, tests/meshopt-simd.test.js, failing on the old source; docs/known-bugs.md). parseKTX2 gives `hasAlpha: false` for KTX2 BC1 RGB (`vkFormat` 131, 132), as the KTX 1 reader already did (tests/ktx2-layers.test.js, failing on the old source). uploadTexture and KTX2GPUDecoder refuse a layer count past `maxTextureArrayLayers` with a RangeError instead of making an invalid texture (tests/ktx2-layers.test.js, tests/ktx2-gpu.test.js, failing on the old source). `createKTX2Texture` and `KTX2Streamer.open` refuse a size past `maxTextureDimension2D`, and the streamer a layer count past `maxTextureArrayLayers`, the same way (tests/decoders.test.js, tests/ktx2-stream.test.js, failing on the old source). KTX2GPUDecoder refuses a UASTC block whose partition pattern is past its mode's table, as it refuses the reserved mode (tests/ktx2-gpu.test.js, failing on the old source). readJXLHeader refuses a container whose signature box is typed `JXL ` but does not hold 0D 0A 87 0A (tests/jxl.test.js, failing on the old source). | DDS DXT1 stays `hasAlpha: true` because the DDS header cannot say which DXT1 kind it holds; no mutation score; the final ISO/IEC 18181-1 header annex and 18181-2 container text are not public and not read; the UASTC HDR 6x6 intermediate format and LZFSE have no published specification |
| cinematic (batch 132 completed 2026-11-15: all 2,618 source lines read; lens.ts, physical-lens.ts, dolly.ts, composer.ts, noise.ts, confine.ts and group.ts's fit read line by line in the earlier pass; this pass read index.ts, target.ts, all of brain.ts and ortho.ts, composer.ts solve, and the dolly.ts and confine.ts pushes line by line) | None fetched: the critically damped spring was checked against the closed-form solution of x'' + 2 w x' + w^2 x = 0, x(t) = (x0 + (v0 + w x0) t) e^(-w t), as Lowe, Critically Damped Ease-In/Ease-Out Smoothing, Game Programming Gems 4 (2004, book) gives it; Perlin's improved noise (2002) quintic fade and Rodrigues' rotation formula from their published forms | index.ts: follow and look springs are that closed form (exact for a goal held over the step); ground clearance per sample t needs dy >= (h + clearance - y0) / t; avoidWalls moves in at once and eases out at the follow rate; brain.ts: choose keeps the shown camera on a tie, advance drops layers a finished one covers, write projects up off the view with the last frame's up as fallback and rolls by u cos r + (f x u) sin r; ortho.ts: right = f x up, screen x = 0.5 + rel.right / (h aspect), y = 0.5 - rel.up / h, group height 2 (|x| + r) / (aspect (1 - 2 padding)); composer solve: height = cy cos p + cz sin p gives p, the horizontal remainder cx R + (cz cos p - cy sin p) H gives the turn, H = h cos t + (up x h) sin t; dolly gap wraps a closed track by half its length, lookAlong turns back at an open end; confine inward normal (-ez, ex) by orientation, margin corner by Cramer on two offset lines. CPU 124/124 (2 new) | 2 defects: (1) brain.ts stepped a disabled camera once per blend layer that showed it, so after a switch back and forth (a, b, a, b) a disabled a ran its stages twice a frame; (2) index.ts raised a below-target orbit's look by the camera's whole height above its arm whenever `ground` was set, so with the ground out of reach a shake or spring lag moved the look-at point | Fixed: (1) each camera of the stack steps once (regression packages/cinematic/test/brain.test.js "a disabled camera shown by two layers of one blend steps once per update"); (2) the raise is capped at what the ground lifted, the arm goal plus the camera lift (regression packages/cinematic/test/cinematic.test.js "a below-target orbit with ground out of reach keeps its look-at point through a shake"); both fail on the old source | Roll blends linearly, so a blend from +170 to -170 degrees of roll turns 340 degrees; kept as authored-angle behaviour. The composer ignores roll and the ground check takes six samples per arm (docs/limits.md). No mutation score |

Third pass, audit-p15: all 11 add-on modules, 2,078 lines (__init__.py, exporter.py, animation.py, objects.py, lightmaps.py, bake.py, nodes.py, curves.py, live.py, writer.py, package_addon.py, with blender_manifest.toml) read line by line against FORMAT.md, glTF 2.0 and the extensions it writes: section table, padding, index format choice and bounds in writer.py; Y-up basis (a proper rotation, so winding and tangent handedness keep), unit scale through convert and the light and camera basis; corner split and deduplication; material subset checks, colour spaces, wrap and filter codes, emission and factor ranges, the clearcoat, sheen, IOR, transmission and subsurface mappings; bake restore paths; light units (683 lm/W over 4 pi sr, sun in lux) and camera fov and ortho fits; skins (bind matrices, weight normalisation, 8 influences), shape keys, action slots, NLA and scene restore in sampling; hierarchy locals for lights and cameras under any parent; curves, points and volumes attribute domains; the live server's loopback and origin rules. The 16 Blender scripts under tools/blender-addon/tests and the 3 pure-Python ones were run, not reviewed line by line: `npm run modern:test:blender` with Blender 5.3.0 Beta passes, every script and browser check | (1) margoLive ignored a live link started again: the add-on's versions count from 1 in each session, and the page only fetched versions above the last one shown, so after Blender was reopened the page stayed on the old scene forever. (2) margoComponents refused `margo:znear` 0 on an orthographic camera, which glTF 2.0 allows (only a perspective znear must be above 0). (3) A node graph whose math operation or mix blend was an Object.prototype name (`constructor`, `toString`, `hasOwnProperty`) crashed with `t.replace is not a function` instead of the coded `unsupported` rejection. Third pass: (4) KB-P15a, TEXCOORD_0, its tangents and Cycles bakes used the UV map selected for editing, while Blender renders with the map marked for rendering, so a mesh whose two flags differ exported every texture misplaced; TEXCOORD_1 was always the second map, the same as TEXCOORD_0 when that one was second. (5) KB-P15b, Spot Blend 0 wrote an inner cone equal to the outer, which the components entry refuses. (6) KB-P15c, a Custom Distance of 0 wrote range 0, refused the same way. (7) The pure-Python add-on tests ran only in the GitHub workflow, which is off, so nothing ran them | (1) Each new connection's first announced version is always loaded (tests/live.test.js, KB-P3a; it hung on the old code). (4) to (6): tools/blender-addon/tests/blender_uv_lights.py, run by `npm run modern:test:blender` (each part fails on the code before): the render map is TEXCOORD_0, the tangent and bake map and the map a live UV Map node must name, the first other map is TEXCOORD_1 and the lightmap target; the inner cone is at most 0.999 of the outer, as the FBX importer has; a zero Custom Distance fails by the light's name. (7) packages/blender/tests/addon-python.test.js runs test_format.py, test_live.py and test_package.py with the package tests. README, docs/limits.md and docs/known-bugs.md updated | parsePreparedPBR's ImageBitmaps leak fixed in an earlier pass. Open: the nodes compiler converts a colour input to a float as the mean of R, G and B, while the Blender manual calls the conversion grayscale; not settled without a Blender probe (listed in the maintainers' bug sweep). LINEAR extrapolation from a CONSTANT end key uses the neighbouring key's slope; not probed against Blender. Spot Blend maps to the inner cone in angle, outer times (1 - blend), and Blender's blend falloff shape differs from glTF's (docs/limits.md); the cone where Blender's falloff starts was not measured. The add-on's test scripts were run, not reviewed line by line; no mutation score |
